- **architect-reviewer**: The engineering lens. - **aries-issue-groomer**: Use FIRST in every /aries-goal cycle to turn the raw `qa-defect` issue queue into an ordered, deduped work list. - **aries-reviewer**: Use as the FINAL gate before any PR, after aries-test-author reports verify green. - **aries-test-author**: Use after an implementer (backend/frontend/integrations) finishes a fix and before review.
